# UX state coverage

Generated by tools/generate-experience-coverage.mjs from registry definitions and successful browser evidence. Each row is a supported screen/platform/scenario association. Scenario definitions supply expected behavior; they are not execution evidence. Only rows linked to an EXP behavioral assertion are marked Verified behavior. The legacy suite additionally smoke-renders 779 screen/scenario associations and exercises core workflows; that evidence does not upgrade every platform-specific row.

“What if something goes wrong?” is available beneath product screens. Payment conditions use the existing payment command and the same reference; a missing checkout must be completed or explicitly replaced with a confirmed example. Other conditions preview the relevant screen and retain existing command guards. Returning to normal preserves recorded effects rather than rolling them back. See [Platform Coverage](Platform-and-Screen-Coverage.md) and [Feature Coverage](Feature-Coverage.md) for applicability and blocked policy decisions.
